                local billboard=Instance.new("BillboardGui",localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui"))
                billboard.Name="VoidUserOverhead"; billboard.Adornee=head
                -- Calamity setupHeadIndicator dimensions and head offset.
                billboard.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(180,64); billboard.StudsOffset=Vector3.new(0,3.4,0)
                billboard.AlwaysOnTop=true; billboard.LightInfluence=0; billboard.MaxDistance=160
                local card=Instance.new("Frame",billboard)
                card.Name="VoidHeadCard"; card.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(2,16)
                Instance.new("UIScale",card).Scale=0.8
                card.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(220,56); card.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(3,3,3)
                card.BackgroundTransparency=0.04; card.BorderSizePixel=0; card.ZIndex=2
                Instance.new("UICorner",card).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,14)
                local border=Instance.new("UIStroke",card)
                border.Color=Color3.fromRGB(183,187,205); border.Thickness=1; border.Transparency=0.58
                local image=Instance.new("ImageLabel",card)
                image.Name="VoidOverheadBackground"; image.Image=bg.Image; image.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
                image.BackgroundTransparency=1; image.ImageTransparency=0.72; image.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Crop
                image.ZIndex=2
                Instance.new("UICorner",image).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,14)
                local rail=Instance.new("Frame",card)
                rail.Position=UDim2.new(0,8,0.5,-15); rail.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(3,30)
                rail.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(194,198,216); rail.BorderSizePixel=0; rail.ZIndex=3
                Instance.new("UICorner",rail).CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)
                local icon=Instance.new("ImageLabel",card)
                icon.Name="VoidOverheadLogo"; icon.Image=win.Logo.Image
                icon.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(19,5); icon.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(13,13)
                icon.BackgroundTransparency=1; icon.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Crop; icon.ZIndex=3
                Instance.new("UICorner",icon).CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)
                local title=Instance.new("TextLabel",card)
                title.Name="UserTag"; title.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(36,7); title.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(105,11)
                title.BackgroundTransparency=1; title.Text=player==localPlayer and "VOID.CC" or "Void.cc user"
                title.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; title.TextSize=8; title.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(155,158,177)
                title.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; title.ZIndex=3
                local link=Instance.new("TextLabel",card)
                link.Name="Discord"; link.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(19,19); link.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(122,25)
                link.BackgroundTransparency=1; link.Text=".gg/buMSD6y9WX"; link.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack
                link.TextSize=14; link.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(245,246,252)
                link.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left; link.ZIndex=3
                local chip=Instance.new("Frame",card)
                chip.Name="SpeedChip"; chip.Position=UDim2.new(1,-72,0,8); chip.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(62,40)
                chip.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(22,23,29); chip.BackgroundTransparency=0.04
                chip.BorderSizePixel=0; chip.ZIndex=3
                gradient(chip,ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,Color3.fromRGB(93,99,125)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.48,Color3.fromRGB(225,230,250)),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,Color3.fromRGB(109,117,148))}),35)
                Instance.new("UICorner",chip).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,10)
                local chipStroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",chip)
                chipStroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(110,113,130); chipStroke.Thickness=0.8; chipStroke.Transparency=0.38
                local caption=Instance.new("TextLabel",chip)
                caption.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0,3); caption.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,10)
                caption.BackgroundTransparency=1; caption.Text="SPEED"; caption.TextSize=7
                caption.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold; caption.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(145,148,166); caption.ZIndex=4
                local speed=Instance.new("TextLabel",chip)
                speed.Name="Speed"; speed.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(0,12); speed.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,23)
                speed.BackgroundTransparency=1; speed.Text="0.0"; speed.TextSize=17
                speed.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; speed.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255); speed.ZIndex=4
                gradient(speed,silver,15)
                -- The identity pointer is separate, so it never enlarges the card.
                local arrow=Instance.new("TextLabel",billboard)
                arrow.Name="VoidPointer"; arrow.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,-12,0,62)
                arrow.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(24,20); arrow.BackgroundTransparency=1
                arrow.Visible=player~=localPlayer
                arrow.Text="▼"; arrow.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack; arrow.TextSize=21
                arrow.TextColor3=Color3.new(0,0,0); arrow.TextStrokeColor3=Color3.fromRGB(208,211,226)
                arrow.TextStrokeTransparency=0.1
                records[player]={gui=billboard,speed=speed,image=image,icon=icon,character=character}
            end)
        end
        local function watch(player,joined)
            if joined and player~=localPlayer and matches(player) then
                win:Notify({Title="Void.cc user arrived",Content=player.DisplayName.." (@"..player.Name..") joined. Void_ prefix detected.",Type="Info",Duration=6})
            end
            if player.Character then build(player,player.Character) end
            characterConnections[player]=player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character) build(player,character) end)
        end
        for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do watch(player,false) end
        track(Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player) watch(player,true) end))
        track(Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
            clear(player)
            if characterConnections[player] then characterConnections[player]:Disconnect(); characterConnections[player]=nil end
        end))
        local elapsed=0
        track(game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            elapsed=elapsed+dt; if elapsed<0.1 then return end; elapsed=0
            for player,record in pairs(records) do
                local root=record.character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root then
                    local velocity=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    local speed=Vector3.new(velocity.X,0,velocity.Z).Magnitude
                    if player==localPlayer and type(_G.__VoidConfiguredSpeed)=="function" then
                        local ok,configured=pcall(_G.__VoidConfiguredSpeed)
                        if ok and type(configured)=="number" and configured==configured and math.abs(configured)<math.huge then
                            speed=configured
                        end
                    end
                    record.speed.Text=string.format("%.1f",speed)
                end
                record.image.Image=bg.Image; record.icon.Image=win.Logo.Image
                if player==localPlayer then
                    local rival=_G.__enemyWidget
                    if rival and rival.Parent then
                        rival.Parent=record.gui
                        rival.AnchorPoint=Vector2.new(0,0)
                        rival.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,-38.5,0,-26)
                        rival.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(140,80)
                        local scale=rival:FindFirstChild("VoidRivalScale") or Instance.new("UIScale",rival)
                        scale.Name="VoidRivalScale"; scale.Scale=0.55
                        rival.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(8,8,13); rival.BackgroundTransparency=0
                        rival.ZIndex=5
                        if not rival:GetAttribute("VoidOverheadStyled") then
                            rival:SetAttribute("VoidOverheadStyled",true)
                            local backdrop=Instance.new("ImageLabel",rival)
                            backdrop.Name="VoidRivalNebula"; backdrop.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
                            backdrop.BackgroundTransparency=1; backdrop.Image=bg.Image; backdrop.ImageTransparency=0.5
                            backdrop.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Crop; backdrop.ZIndex=600
                            Instance.new("UICorner",backdrop).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,8)
                            local rim=rival:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke") or Instance.new("UIStroke",rival)
                            rim.Color=Color3.fromRGB(205,213,245); rim.Thickness=1.5
                            gradient(rim,silver,25)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end))
        _G.__BubbleTrackStopper(function()
            alive=false
            for _,connection in pairs(characterConnections) do connection:Disconnect() end
            for player in pairs(records) do clear(player) end
        end)
    end

    -- Reuse the original live controls instead of approximating their handlers.
    local nativeNames={Movement="MovementPage",Combat="CombatPage",Visuals="VisualPage"}
    local function mountOriginal(sub,pageName)
        local page=engine.panel:FindFirstChild(pageName,true)
        if not page or not page:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then return false end
        sub._card:Destroy()
        for _,padding in ipairs(sub._page:GetChildren()) do if padding:IsA("UIPadding") then padding:Destroy() end end
        page.Parent=sub._page; page.Position=UDim2.fromScale(0,0); page.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1)
        page.Visible=true; page.BackgroundTransparency=1
        sub._page.ScrollingEnabled=false; sub._page.AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.None
        sub._page.CanvasSize=UDim2.new(); sub._card=page
        for _,d in ipairs(page:GetDescendants()) do
            if d:IsA("TextLabel") and d.Text=="Auto Steal" then d.Text="Insta Steal" end
        end
        return true
    end
    for _,category in ipairs(categories) do
        local sub=win:AddTab({Name=category[1],Icon=category[2]}):AddSubTab("Controls")
        local native=nativeNames[category[1]] and mountOriginal(sub,nativeNames[category[1]])
        if not native then for _,key in ipairs(category[3]) do addToggle(sub,key) end end
        if category[1]=="Combat" then
            local before={}; for _,child in ipairs(sub._card:GetChildren()) do before[child]=true end
            table.insert(modeSelectors,sub:AddDropdown({Name="Auto Grab Mode",Options={"V1","V2","V3","V4"},Default="V4",
                Callback=function(mode) chooseGrabMode(mode) end}))
            sub:AddToggle({Name="Show Grab Bar",Description="Compact draggable Insta Steal pill",Default=_G.__VoidGrabBarEnabled==true,
                Callback=function(value) _G.__VoidGrabBarEnabled=value; pill.Visible=value end})
            local settings=sub._tab:AddSubTab("Grab Settings")
            table.insert(modeSelectors,settings:AddDropdown({Name="Auto Grab Mode",Options={"V1","V2","V3","V4"},Default=selectedGrabMode,
                Callback=function(mode) chooseGrabMode(mode) end}))
            local rootCard=settings._card
            local function group(mode,title)
                local box=Instance.new("Frame",rootCard)
                box.Name="GrabSettings_"..mode; box.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,0)
                box.AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y; box.BackgroundTransparency=1
                box.Visible=selectedGrabMode==mode; box.LayoutOrder=100
                local layout=Instance.new("UIListLayout",box); layout.Padding=UDim.new(0,8)
                layout.SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
                modeGroups[mode]=box; settings._card=box
                settings:AddSection({Name=title})
            end
            local numberHandles={}
            local function number(name,key,mn,mx)
                local handle
                handle=settings:AddInput({Name=name,Default=tostring(grabSettings[key]),Placeholder=tostring(mn).."–"..tostring(mx),
                    Callback=function(text)
                        local value=tonumber((tostring(text):gsub(",",".")))
                        if not value or value~=value or math.abs(value)==math.huge then handle:Set(grabSettings[key]); return end
                        value=math.clamp(value,mn,mx)
                        grabSettings[key]=value
                        if key=="holdMin" and value>grabSettings.holdMax then grabSettings.holdMax=value end
                        if key=="holdMax" and value<grabSettings.holdMin then grabSettings.holdMin=value end
                        for field,handles in pairs(numberHandles) do
                            for _,input in ipairs(handles) do input:Set(grabSettings[field]) end
                        end
                        calGrab.SetSettings(grabSettings)
                    end})
                numberHandles[key]=numberHandles[key] or {}; table.insert(numberHandles[key],handle)
            end
            group("V1","V1 / Normal grab")
            number("Grab Radius","radius",0.5,300)
            number("Hold Duration (s)","duration",0.1,10)
            settings:AddToggle({Name="Auto Radius",Description="Configured movement speed + 1",Default=grabSettings.autoRadius,
                Callback=function(on) grabSettings.autoRadius=on; calGrab.SetSettings(grabSettings) end})
            group("V2","V2 / Semi grab")
            number("Semi Radius (max 10)","semiRadius",0.5,10)
            number("Hold Min (s)","holdMin",0.1,5)
            number("Hold Max (s)","holdMax",0.1,8)
            group("V3","V3 / Hold-and-fill grab")
            number("Grab Radius","radius",0.5,300)
            number("Fill Duration (s)","duration",0.1,10)
            number("Stop Time (s)","stopTime",0.1,30)
            group("V4","V4 / Original grab")
            settings:AddInput({Name="Grab Radius",Default=tostring(engine.grabRadius()),Callback=function(text)
                local value=tonumber(text); if value and value==value and math.abs(value)<math.huge then engine.setGrabRadius(math.clamp(value,10,150)) end
            end})
            settings._card=rootCard

            for _,child in ipairs(sub._card:GetChildren()) do if not before[child] and child:IsA("GuiObject") then child.LayoutOrder=10000 end end
        end
    end
    for _,item in ipairs({{"Animations","AnimationsPage","film"},{"Keybinds","KeybindsPage","keyboard"}}) do
        local sub=win:AddTab({Name=item[1],Icon=item[3]}):AddSubTab("Original controls")
        mountOriginal(sub,item[2])
    end

    if win.Logo and win.Logo.Parent then

    end
    -- Decorate existing quick/toggle/action buttons; do not replace connections.
    local buttonImages={}
    local function styleButton(button)
        if not button:IsA("TextButton") or button:GetAttribute("VoidStyled") then return end
        if button.Name=="BubbleRestoreButton" or button.Name=="ToggleInstaSteal" then return end
        -- Empty full-row hit targets must remain transparent; style their row instead.
        if button.BackgroundTransparency>=1 and button.Text=="" then return end
        button:SetAttribute("VoidStyled",true)
        if button:FindFirstAncestor("BubbleQuickButtons") then
            button.BackgroundTransparency=0
            track(button:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundTransparency"):Connect(function()
                if button.BackgroundTransparency~=0 then button.BackgroundTransparency=0 end
            end))
        end
        local g=button:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient") or Instance.new("UIGradient",button)
        g.Color=silver; g.Rotation=25; animatedGradients[g]=25
        button.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(36,37,45)
        button.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(244,245,252)
        if not button:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner") then Instance.new("UICorner",button).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,7) end
        local stroke=button:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke") or Instance.new("UIStroke",button)
        stroke.ApplyStrokeMode=Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border; stroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(151,155,176); stroke.Thickness=1
        local image=Instance.new("ImageLabel",button)
        image.Name="VoidButtonNebula"; image.Image=bg.Image; image.BackgroundTransparency=1
        image.ImageTransparency=0.9; image.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        image.Size=UDim2.fromScale(1,1); image.ZIndex=button.ZIndex; image.Active=false
        local round=Instance.new("UICorner",image); round.CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,7)
        table.insert(buttonImages,image)
    end
    local function styleTree(root)
        for _,child in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do styleButton(child) end
        track(root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(child) task.defer(function() if child.Parent then styleButton(child) end end) end))
    end
    styleTree(win.ScreenGui)
    styleTree(engine.gui)
    local playerGui=game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if playerGui and playerGui~=engine.gui.Parent then
        local mobile=playerGui:FindFirstChild("BubbleMobileUI")
        if mobile then styleTree(mobile) end
    end
    -- Floating logo button also toggles the main window; feature quick buttons
    -- retain their native feature-toggle/action callbacks and native drag handling.
    local menu=Instance.new("ImageButton",grabGui)
    menu.Name="VoidMenuToggle"; menu.Size=UDim2.fromOffset(44,44); menu.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(12,54)
    menu.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(22,22,28); menu.Image=bg.Image; menu.ImageTransparency=0.35
    menu.AutoButtonColor=false
    Instance.new("UICorner",menu).CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)
    gradient(menu,silver,30)
    local rim=Instance.new("UIStroke",menu); rim.Thickness=1.5; rim.Color=Color3.fromRGB(196,199,216)
    local mark=Instance.new("ImageLabel",menu); mark.Size=UDim2.new(1,-8,1,-8); mark.Position=UDim2.fromOffset(4,4)
    mark.BackgroundTransparency=1; mark.Image=win.Logo.Image; mark.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Crop
    Instance.new("UICorner",mark).CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)
    track(menu.Activated:Connect(function() win:ToggleUI() end))
    track(bg:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Image"):Connect(function()
        menu.Image=bg.Image
        for _,image in ipairs(buttonImages) do if image.Parent then image.Image=bg.Image end end
    end))
    track(win.Logo:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Image"):Connect(function() mark.Image=win.Logo.Image end))

    -- Hide the legacy panel, but retain feature widgets and original callbacks.
    local function hideLegacy()
        if engine.panel and engine.panel.Parent then engine.panel.Visible=false end
        for _,d in ipairs(engine.gui:GetChildren()) do
            if d:IsA("GuiObject") and (d.Name=="BubbleRestoreButton" or d.Name=="LegacyHeaderStub") then d.Visible=false end
        end
    end
    hideLegacy()
    if engine.panel then track(engine.panel:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(hideLegacy)) end
    track(engine.gui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
        if d:IsA("GuiObject") and d.Name=="BubbleRestoreButton" then d.Visible=false end
    end))
    engine.actions["Toggle UI"]=function() win:ToggleUI() end
    _G.__setPanelVisible=function(visible) win:SetUIVisible(visible) end
    -- Original keybinds and quick buttons can change state independently.
    local elapsed=0
    track(game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
        elapsed=elapsed+dt; if elapsed<0.2 then return end; elapsed=0
        if not win.ScreenGui.Parent then return end
        syncing=true
        for key,handle in pairs(handles) do if handle:Get()~=engine.state[key] then handle:Set(engine.state[key]) end end
        syncing=false
        hideLegacy()
    end))
    _G.__BubbleTrackStopper(function()
        for _,conn in ipairs(conns) do conn:Disconnect() end
        win:Destroy()
        if _G.__VOID_DUELS_WINDOW==win then _G.__VOID_DUELS_WINDOW=nil end
    end)
    -- Make cards translucent after creating all Oxide controls.
    for _,d in ipairs(win.ScreenGui:GetDescendants()) do
        if d:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then
            for _,card in ipairs(d:GetChildren()) do
                if card:IsA("Frame") and card.AutomaticSize==Enum.AutomaticSize.Y then card.BackgroundTransparency=0.3 end
            end
        end
    end
end

                                            local function o(r)
                                                if not r then
                                                    return nil;
                                                end;
                                                if type(r) == "number" then
                                                    return "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(r);
                                                end;
                                                if type(r) == "string" then
                                                    local Ki = string.gsub(r, "^%s+", "");
                                                    Ki = string.gsub(Ki, "%s+$", "");
                                                    if Ki == "" then
                                                        return nil;
                                                    end;
                                                    if string.sub(Ki, 1, 11) == "rbxassetid://" then
                                                        return Ki;
